## 项目背景
项目根目录（虚拟路径 `/`）下有两个文件：
- `/auth.py`：核心认证逻辑 + 命令行入口
- `/test_auth.py`：unittest 测试

需求约束（审查时以此为准）：
1. 只用 Python 标准库 + SQLite，不得有第三方依赖。
2. 用户数据存 SQLite（users.db），密码必须加盐哈希存储，使用 `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac`，禁止明文/MD5/SHA1。
3. `register(username, password)`：用户名唯一、密码最少 8 位、用户名非空；重复用户名抛明确异常。
4. `login(username, password)`：成功 True，失败 False；用户不存在与密码错误不得泄露具体原因。
5. CLI 支持 `python3 auth.py register &lt;username&gt;` 和 `python3 auth.py login &lt;username&gt;`，密码通过 `getpass` 交互输入，不得放在命令行参数里。
6. unittest 测试覆盖：注册成功、重复注册失败、登录成功、密码错误、密码过短。

## 审查重点（请逐项给出结论）
1. **密码存储**：是否真的加盐、盐是否随机且每用户独立、迭代次数是否合理、是否用了 pbkdf2_hmac、是否存在明文/MD5/SHA1 残留、哈希比较是否常量时间（hmac.compare_digest）。
2. **SQL 注入**：所有 SQL 是否参数化，有无字符串拼接/格式化 SQL，表名或字段名是否被动态拼接。
3. **异常处理**：异常类型是否明确、是否吞掉异常、CLI 是否会打印 traceback、是否有裸 except、重复用户名是否靠错误字符串匹配（脆弱）。
4. **边界情况**：空/纯空白用户名、超长用户名、超长密码、Unicode 密码、密码恰好 8 位、用户名大小写、并发写入、数据库文件权限、db 路径可配置性、首次建表逻辑、`login` 对不存在用户的行为与信息泄露、时序攻击防护是否有效。
5. **测试质量**：是否真的用临时数据库、是否可能污染真实 users.db、断言是否有效（有没有假通过）、覆盖是否满足要求、有无遗漏的关键用例。
6. **CLI 正确性**：密码是否确实走 getpass、参数校验、退出码、usage 提示。

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""A small user authentication system backed by SQLite.

Only the Python standard library is used. Passwords are stored as salted
PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 hashes; every user gets an independent random salt.

Usage:
    python3 auth.py register &lt;username&gt;
    python3 auth.py login &lt;username&gt;

The password is always read interactively with getpass, never from argv.
"""

import getpass
import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import secrets
import sqlite3
import sys

# --- Hashing parameters -----------------------------------------------------

PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 200_000
HASH_ALGORITHM = "sha256"
SALT_BYTES = 16
MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8

DEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "users.db")


# --- Exceptions -------------------------------------------------------------


class AuthError(Exception):
    """Base class for authentication errors."""


class UserAlreadyExistsError(AuthError):
    """Raised when registering a username that is already taken."""


class ValidationError(AuthError, ValueError):
    """Raised when a username or password fails validation."""


# --- Database helpers -------------------------------------------------------


def get_db_path(db_path=None):
    """Resolve the database path.

    Priority: explicit argument &gt; AUTH_DB_PATH env var &gt; users.db next to this file.
    """
    if db_path:
        return db_path
    return os.environ.get("AUTH_DB_PATH") or DEFAULT_DB_PATH


def _connect(db_path=None):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(get_db_path(db_path))
    conn.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            salt BLOB NOT NULL,
            password_hash BLOB NOT NULL,
            iterations INTEGER NOT NULL,
            algorithm TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
        )
        """
    )
    conn.commit()
    return conn


# --- Hashing helpers --------------------------------------------------------


def _hash_password(password, salt, iterations=PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, algorithm=HASH_ALGORITHM):
    return hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(algorithm, password.encode("utf-8"), salt, iterations)


def _validate_credentials(username, password):
    if not isinstance(username, str) or not username.strip():
        raise ValidationError("Username must not be empty.")
    if not isinstance(password, str) or len(password) &lt; MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH:
        raise ValidationError(
            "Password must be at least %d characters long." % MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH
        )


# --- Public API -------------------------------------------------------------


def register(username, password, db_path=None):
    """Register a new user.

"""Tests for auth.py. Run with: python3 -m unittest discover -v"""

import os
import sqlite3
import tempfile
import unittest

import auth


class AuthTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        fd, self.db_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        os.unlink(self.db_path)  # let sqlite create it fresh

    def tearDown(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.db_path):
            os.unlink(self.db_path)

    def _fetch(self, username):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        try:
            return conn.execute(
                "SELECT salt, password_hash, iterations, algorithm FROM users WHERE username = ?",
                (username,),
            ).fetchone()
        finally:
            conn.close()

    # --- required cases -----------------------------------------------------

    def test_register_success(self):
        user_id = auth.register("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        self.assertIsInstance(user_id, int)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self._fetch("alice"))

    def test_register_duplicate_raises(self):
        auth.register("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        with self.assertRaises(auth.UserAlreadyExistsError):
            auth.register("alice", "another password", db_path=self.db_path)

    def test_login_success(self):
        auth.register("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        self.assertTrue(auth.login("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path))

    def test_login_wrong_password(self):
        auth.register("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        self.assertFalse(auth.login("alice", "wrong horse", db_path=self.db_path))

    def test_short_password_raises(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            auth.register("alice", "short", db_path=self.db_path)

    # --- extra cases --------------------------------------------------------

    def test_login_unknown_user(self):
        self.assertFalse(auth.login("nobody", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path))

    def test_empty_username_raises(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            auth.register("", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            auth.register("   ", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)

    def test_username_is_stripped(self):
        auth.register("  alice  ", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path)
        self.assertTrue(auth.login("alice", "correct horse", db_path=self.db_path))

    def test_password_not_stored_in_plaintext(self):
        password = "correct horse"
        auth.register("alice", password, db_path=self.db_path)
        salt, password_hash, iterations, algorithm = self._fetch("alice")
        self.assertNotEqual(password_hash, password.encode("utf-8"))
        self.assertNotIn(password.encode("utf-8"), password_hash)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(iterations, 100_000)
        self.assertEqual(algorithm, "sha256")
        self.assertEqual(len(salt), auth.SALT_BYTES)

    def test_salts_are_random(self):
        auth.register("alice", "same password", db_path=self.db_path)
        auth.register("bob", "same password", db_path=self.db_path)
        alice = self._fetch("alice")
        bob = self._fetch("bob")
        self.assertNotEqual(alice[0], bob[0])
        self.assertNotEqual(alice[1], bob[1])

    def test_db_path_from_env(self):
        old = os.environ.get("AUTH_DB_PATH")
        os.environ["AUTH_DB_PATH"] = self.db_path
        try:
            auth.register("alice", "correct horse")
            self.assertTrue(auth.login("alice", "correct horse"))
        finally:
            if old is None:
                os.environ.pop("AUTH_DB_PATH", None)
            else:
                os.environ["AUTH_DB_PATH"] = old

            )
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
            raise UserAlreadyExistsError("Username %r is already taken." % username)
        conn.commit()
        return cursor.lastrowid
    finally:
        conn.close()


def login(username, password, db_path=None):
    """Return True if the credentials are valid, False otherwise.

    A missing user and a wrong password are indistinguishable to the caller.
    """
    if not isinstance(username, str) or not isinstance(password, str):
        return False

    conn = _connect(db_path)
    try:
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT salt, password_hash, iterations, algorithm FROM users WHERE username = ?",
            (username.strip(),),
        ).fetchone()
    finally:
        conn.close()

    if row is None:
        # Perform an equivalent hash computation so that a missing user does
        # not stand out through timing.
        _hash_password(password, b"\x00" * SALT_BYTES)
        return False

    salt, stored_hash, iterations, algorithm = row
    candidate = _hash_password(password, salt, iterations, algorithm)
    return hmac.compare_digest(candidate, stored_hash)


# --- Command line interface -------------------------------------------------

USAGE = """Usage:
  python3 auth.py register &lt;username&gt;
  python3 auth.py login &lt;username&gt;

The password is read interactively (never passed on the command line)."""


def _cmd_register(username):
    password = getpass.getpass("Password: ")
    confirm = getpass.getpass("Confirm password: ")
    if password != confirm:
        print("Error: passwords do not match.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    try:
        user_id = register(username, password)
    except UserAlreadyExistsError as exc:
        print("Error: %s" % exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except ValidationError as exc:
        print("Error: %s" % exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        print("Database error: %s" % exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print("User %r registered successfully (id=%d)." % (username.strip(), user_id))
    return 0


def _cmd_login(username):
    password = getpass.getpass("Password: ")
    try:
        ok = login(username, password)
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        print("Database error: %s" % exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    if ok:
        print("Login successful. Welcome, %s!" % username.strip())
        return 0
    print("Login failed: invalid username or password.", file=sys.stderr)
    return 1


def main(argv=None):
    argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
    if len(argv) != 2 or argv[0] not in ("register", "login"):
        print(USAGE, file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    command, username = argv
    if command == "register":
        return _cmd_register(username)
    return _cmd_login(username)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())</message>
